日本：旺盛なAI関連需要が製造業の景況感を押し上げ。利益計画は大幅上方修正
3
 大企業の業況判断は製造業が6四半期連続の改善。非製造業は悪化も一時的な悪天候が主因で変調の懸念は低い
― 業種別では、化学、石油・石炭製品、生産用機械等が改善。旺盛なAI関連需要や原油の供給不足懸念の後退等が背景

― 一方、非製造業は、長雨を受けて小売や対個人サービス、宿泊・飲食サービスが悪化
 2026年度の経常利益計画は、製造業中心に大幅上方修正。9月調査時点としては過去5年間で最高の増益計画
― とりわけ大企業・製造業では、輸出向け売上高の上方修正が顕著。上期の実績が大幅に上振れたことを反映したもの
― 世界的なAI関連需要の好調持続が見込まれるもと、下期も堅調な輸出が続けば、利益計画は一段の上方修正余地

日本：設備投資は高水準を維持。製造業は業績対比でなお上振れ余地
4
 2026年度の設備投資計画は前年度比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持
― 製造業は6月調査からほぼ横ばいの同＋7.1％。非製造業は同＋11.8％と1.1％Ptの上方修正
 AI需要による好業績を踏まえれば製造業の投資計画にはやや慎重さがにじむ内容
― 一方、生産・営業用設備判断DIは2025年度後半以降、設備不足方向への動きが鮮明。高水準の内部資金も踏まえれ
ば、増産投資などを中心に今後の設備投資が上振れる余地
― なお、今後の利上げが企業の投資姿勢に及ぼす影響には留意
25年度後半以降、急速
な設備不足超を示唆

日本：企業の価格設定姿勢はなお強い。仕入価格再上昇にも警戒
5
 大企業・製造業の販売価格判断DIは前期並みの高水準を維持。企業は積極的な価格転嫁姿勢を継続
―6・7月の油価下落等を背景に素材業種の仕入価格判断DIが低下。対して販売価格判断DIは横ばい。コスト低下局面で
も販売価格を維持しており、企業利益の押し上げ要因に
 企業の中長期的な物価見通しも2％台半ばと、日銀の物価目標を上回る水準で高止まり
― 足元の原油価格再上昇やタンカー運賃高騰を受け、仕入価格が再び上昇する可能性には留意。企業の価格設定姿勢
が一段と強まるリスクも
販売価格判断DI・仕入価格判断DI（大企業・製造業） 物価全般の見通し（全規模・全産業）

日本：利上げでも企業金融は安定。企業部門の金利耐性を確認
6
 資金繰り判断は総じて底堅い。これまでの利上げの影響は限定的
― 利上げの影響が懸念された中小企業の資金繰り判断は横ばい圏の推移。価格転嫁の進展などを受けた事業環境の改
善が資金繰りを支えている構図
― 金利上昇局面でも銀行貸出は増加基調を維持しており、企業の資金需要も底堅く推移
 企業収益や資金繰りが金利上昇への耐性を示す中、金融環境はなお緩和的
― 市場のターミナルレート予想は2.5％程度まで上昇し、日銀の中立金利推計レンジ上限に接近

（%）
欧州：G7は原油・ディーゼルの協調放出で合意。米国の輸出制限リスクは後退
7
 G7はIEAを通じ4カ月で1億バレルの石油備蓄放出に合意。初期20日間はディーゼルを前倒し放出
― 背景にディーゼル価格高騰。中間選挙を控えたトランプ大統領は、禁輸も辞さない姿勢を強調（米国は世界需要の4.9％
を供給）。米国からの輸出が欧州の需要を支える一方、米国よりも潤沢な在庫を保有する欧州に圧力をかけた格好
― 欧州は石油製品消費に占めるディーゼル比率が高く、価格高騰を回避する思惑も協調放出の合意を後押し
― 合意ではG7域内でのエネルギー輸出制限回避も確認され、米国によるEU向け禁輸リスクはひとまず後退
石油製品の製品別消費割合の国際比較
（出所）Energy Institute より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
EUの石油製品消費はディーゼル・軽油が中心

### Page 9

欧州：ディーゼル需給逼迫は残存。欧州のインフレ上振れ要因に
8
 ロシアのディーゼル輸出は足元で急減。中東からの輸出減、中国の輸出停止も重なり、世界需要の約6％が失われた格好
―G7の1億バレル放出で当面の供給不安は緩和するものの、構造的な供給不足の解消には至らず
―IEAも、中東からの原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品の供給制約はなお深刻と指摘。ディーゼル需給の逼迫が続け
ば、輸送・物流コストを通じたインフレ圧力が残存
 欧州では9月HICPが再加速。ディーゼル高が輸送・物流コストを通じて追加的なインフレ上振れ要因となるリスク
―9月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋3.8％（前月：同＋3.2％）と伸び加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が主因
今般の備蓄放出量と停止中のディーゼル輸出
（注） 世界需要およびロシア・中国の輸出量は2025年実績。中東は2026年8月実績を参
考に、輸出量を前年の4分の1としてシェアを概算
（出所）EIA、IEA、OPEC、各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

注目点②G7備蓄放出でもくすぶるディーゼル供給不安

米国：雇用は減速も、AI関連需要が下支え
 9月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋2.9万人と市場予想（同＋9.0万人）を下回る結果。7月・8月も下方修正
― 民間部門が前月差＋4.6万人（8月同+8.9万人）と増勢が鈍化したほか、政府部門が同▲1.7万人と再びマイナス転化
― 民間部門では、旺盛なAI投資需要などを背景に、製造や建設、建設・エンジニアリングなどが増加基調を維持したもの
の、情報、金融、専門サービスなどの高賃金セクターが減少
― 時間当たり賃金の伸びは前月比＋0.1％（8月同＋0.3％）と鈍化。こちらも市場予想（同＋0.3％）を下回る結果
― 総じて冴えない結果ながら、3カ月平均では＋5万人程度を維持しており、雇用は減速しつつも失速には至らず

9
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：対人型業種の雇用に底堅さ。労働参加率上昇で失業率は小幅上昇
 対人接触度の高い業種では雇用に底堅さ、AIによる代替が進みにくい可能性
― ヘルスケアや建設、飲食サービスなど、対人接触機会の多い業種では雇用が増加する一方、経理・会計サービス、ITシ
ステム開発などでは低調
 失業率は4.2％と小幅悪化も引き続き低水準を維持
― 主に16-24歳の若年層およびプライムエイジ層の労働参加率の上昇が押し上げに寄与した模様

中国：景況感は急速に改善。建設業が持ち直し。政府は住宅ローンの利子補給で市場テコ入れ
 9月の総合PMIは50.7と、前月（49.5）から大幅に上昇。景況感が急速に改善。建設業が大きく持ち直し
― 製造業PMIが50.1（前月：49.8）、非製造業PMIが50.2（前月：49.0）とともに節目回復。製造業の生産（51.7）が堅調
― 建設業PMIは50.3と、前月（46.9）から急上昇。異常気象の影響はく落と財政予算執行の本格化があるとみられる
 中国政府が9/28、新規住宅ローン金利の年率1％分を補助する政策を発表。低迷が続く不動産市場をテコ入れ
― 対象物件の購入金額を150万元（3,500万円）以下に限定し、低中所得者層の住宅ローン負担を軽減する
― これにより、余剰在庫に苦しむ地方都市での実需を掘り起こし、市況のさらなる悪化に歯止めをかけたい考え

### Page 13

 中国のASEAN向け輸出品は、ASEANの製造品と競合。競合分野は一般機械や玩具、食品など幅広に
― 一般機械等に加え、ベトナムは電子機器や紙・木・繊維等の軽工業品、インドネシアは輸送機械でも競合度が高い
 中国製品流入が強まる中、ASEANはアンチダンピング、セーフガード措置に加え、越境ECへの規制強化などで対応
― 素材・中間財についてはアンチダンピング措置で対応。最終財では、低価格輸入品への課税や価格規制で応じる方向
― 中国企業等による規制回避を懸念し、ASEAN側が対象品目拡張や反迂回措置を強化する動きもあるが、こうした措置
の実効性は不透明
アジア：中国製品との競争激化でASEANの防衛策が拡大

 2026年以降に相違が目立つ台湾と韓国におけるAI需要の恩恵

日本：コアCPIは政策補助効果のはく落を主因に加速。その背後で徐々に高まるインフレ圧力
14
 9月の都区部コアCPIは前年比＋2.7％（前月：同＋1.8％）と大幅加速。政策要因のはく落に加え、価格転嫁の影響も拡大
― 東京都で実施された水道基本料金無償化・保育所保育料無償化の影響がはく落し、全体の伸びを＋0.56％Pt押し上げ

## Document 055: 2026年9月社融数据点评：政府债融资支撑增强，政策成效静待释放

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月社融数据点评：政府债融资支撑增强，政策成效静待释放.pdf
Report source: 西南证券
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分  
1 
ooo[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 10 月 16 日      
证 券研究报告 •宏观简评报告      
数据点评  
政府 债融资支撑 增强 ，政策成效静待 释放 
——2026 年 9 月社融数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院  
[Table_Summary]  摘要：总体来看，9 月社会融资规模存量同比增长7.1%，增速较上月放缓0.1
个百分点，单月新增社融 3.55 万亿元，同比多增 177 亿元，政府债券融资大
幅多增是主要支撑。9 月政府债券融资新增 1.54万亿元， 同比多增3469亿元，
专项债发行明显提速， 前期政策性金融工具陆续投放， 财政托底作用逐步显现；
但 1—9 月政府债券净融资仍同比少增约1.16 万亿元， 全年专项债发行进度略
慢于去年， 四季度政府债融资仍有支撑。企业债券融资单月转负， 净融资减少
393 亿元，同比多减529 亿元，但 1-9 月累计净融资同比多增约1.18万亿元，
直接融资仍有一定韧性。 间接融资方面，9 月人民币贷款增加 1.27万亿元， 同
比少增 3347 亿元。企业中长期贷款同比少增幅度明显收窄，政策性金融工具
对配套融资的带动作用开始显现， 但企业投资需求仍偏弱； 居民贷款增量为0，
消费需求不足、 房地产成交走弱及提前还贷继续制约居民信贷扩张。 展望后续，
政府债融资及政策性金融工具有望继续支撑社融，企业中长期贷款或边际改
善，但居民信贷修复仍需时间，社融改善仍较依赖政策发力。存款端，9 月人
民币存款同比少增 4400 亿元，居民存款少增、非银存款同比多减，反映居民
资金配置及季末资金流动变化。9 月末 M2、M1 同比分别增长 7.2%、3.2%，
增速均有所回落，M2-M1 剪刀差扩大至 4.0 个百分点， 货币活化程度有所下降。
后续财政资金拨付和配套融资落地有望支撑企业存款， 但信贷增速下行仍制约
货币派生，预计 M2 保持一定韧性，M1 修复仍取决于实体需求改善。 
 社融存量增速放缓，增量政策效应有待释放。从社融存量看，2026年 9 月，
社会融资规模存量同比上升 7.1%，增速相比上月放缓 0.1 个百分点。从社融
增量看，9 月单月的社会融资规模增量为 3.55 万亿元，同比多增 177 亿元。9
月单月的人民币贷款增加 1.27万亿元， 同比少增 3347亿元。本月社融同比多
增主要由政府债券融资大幅多增带动， 反映出专项债发行提速、 前期政策性金
融工具陆续投放， 财政对实体经济的支持作用逐步显现。 从信贷结构看， 企业
贷款同比多增， 主要受企业中长期贷款和票据融资同比少减支撑， 低基数效应
亦有一定影响。居民贷款则继续承压，消费需求偏弱、房地产成交表现不足制
约居民加杠杆意愿，叠加提前还贷等因素，居民信贷需求仍显不足。9 月 29
日，货币与财政政策同步推出增量措施，央行下调PSL 利率并扩大支持领域，
同时增加科技创新、技术改造和支农支小再贷款额度；财政自10 月 1 日起对
符合条件的新发放首套住房商业性个人住房贷款实施贴息。两项政策有望通过
支持企业投资、 改善居民购房需求，带动企业中长期贷款和居民中长期贷款增
长，并通过财政资金投放及配套融资对社融形成支撑。四季度社融增速有望在
政府债放量与 PSL、 房贷贴息等政策工具协同发力下保持平稳， 结构上或逐步
从“政府驱动”向“政企协同”过渡。企业和居民中长期贷款有望边际改善，
但信贷需求修复仍需时间。 
 财政托底支撑政府债融资，企业债融资季节性回落。从社融结构看，2026年 9
月，直接融资新增1.56 万亿元，同比多增 3099亿元，主要受政府债券融资支
撑。从分项看，政府债融资方面，9 月政府债融资新增1.54 万亿元，同比多增
3469 亿元。今年 1-9 月，政府债券净融资约10.30万亿元， 同比仍少增约1.16

万亿元，显示单月融资支撑增强，但累计规模仍低于去年同期。今年财政前置
发力节奏与去年形成差异， 超长期特别国债和新增专项债在9 月迎来阶段性集
中发行，政府债券净融资对社融增速的拉动力度有所提升。截至 10 月 16 日，
今年 1.3 万亿元超长期特别国债全部发行完毕，进度与去年持平。9 月单月，
地方政府新增专项债发行 6204 亿元，同比涨幅扩大至 50.18%，发行进度
80.62%，略慢于去年同期。专项债三季度发行节奏明显提速，单季发行规模
创年内新高，但全年进度仍略慢于去年，主要原因是项目储备与资金需求之间
的匹配存在时滞。企业直接融资方面，9 月单月企业债券净融资减少 393 亿元，
同比多减 529 亿元。企业债券融资单月转负， 一方面源于9 月为信用债发行传
统淡季， 季节性因素带动发行规模回落；另一方面也由于此前企业债融资需求
集中释放后，市场进入阶段性休整。但 1-9 月企业债券净融资约 2.75万亿元，
同比多增约 1.18 万亿元，单月表现偏弱与累计融资改善并存。非金融企业境

内股票融资同比多增159 亿元至 658 亿元， 股权融资延续改善态势。表外融资
方面，表外融资增加 2479 亿元，同比少增 1100 亿元。其中，委托贷款增加
804 亿元，同比多增 521 亿元，9 月新型政策性金融工具落地为委托贷款提供
了增量支撑。信托贷款减少 478 亿元， 同比多减 540 亿元。未贴现银行承兑汇
票新增 2153 亿元，同比少增 1081 亿元。银行季末票据冲量推动票据融资从
表外转向表内，未贴现票据同比少增，主要是表内票据对表外融资替代所致。
往后看，政府债方面，截至 10月 15 日，今年地方政府专项债已发行 3.64 万
亿元，年内剩余发行额度为 7592 亿元。结合 10 月 9日公布的 5500亿元地方
政府债务限额盘活安排，四季度政府债融资仍有一定支撑。跨季后理财资金回
流、到期偿付压力减轻， 企业债净融资有望由负转正。委托贷款受益于新型政
策性金融工具“股东借款”的投放，或将延续多增。
 低基数支撑票据融资，消费与地产低迷拖累居民信贷。企业端，2026年 9 月，

企（事）业单位贷款增加 1.29 万亿元，同比多增 700 亿元。其中，企业短期
贷款增加 4600 亿元，同比少增 2500 亿元，主要源于去年短贷冲量形成的高
基数，叠加票据融资同比大幅少减，对短贷形成替代效应。企业中长期贷款增
加 8900 亿元，同比少增 200 亿元，但少增幅度已由上月的1500 亿元收窄至
200 亿元。这一改善主要源于当月政府债券供给增加、政策性金融工具对配套
贷款的撬动作用开始显现。然而在投资需求偏弱背景下， 企业扩张性融资意愿
仍显不足， 中长期信贷尚未形成有效支撑。票据融资减少 500 亿元， 同比少减
3526 亿元，主要受去年同期低基数影响（去年同期净减少4026亿元）， 同时
也体现银行季末票据冲量行为。非银贷款减少1485亿元，同比少减 863 亿元。
居民端，9 月居民贷款增量为 0， 同比多减 3890亿元。 其中， 居民短期贷款减
少 900 亿元，同比多减 2321 亿元，主要与居民消费及分期需求偏弱有关。9
月狭义乘用车零售同比降约 28%，大件消费对短贷的支撑不足；同时银行防

控风险主动收缩信用卡、消费贷供给，也对短贷形成压制。居民中长期贷款增
加 874 亿元，同比少增 1626 亿元。9 月我国 30 大中城市成交面积为652.33
万平方米，同比下降 10.75%，降幅较上月扩大 4.44个百分点，房价与收入预
期尚未趋势性改善，制约了居民加杠杆意愿。往后看，新型政策性金融工具投
放有望带动配套贷款增加， 叠加去年同期低基数， 企业中长期贷款同比或将修
复；但 10 月表内票据迎来到期高峰，可能对信贷总量形成对冲。居民端，购
房贴息政策落地带动一线成交回暖， 但低线需求偏弱， 居民中长期贷款难以大
幅回升。综合来看，10月新增信贷同比或仍少增， 结构改善优先于总量回升。
 存款结构分化加剧，货币活化程度下降。从存款端来看，2026年 9 月，人民
币存款增加 17700 亿元，同比少增 4400 亿元。分存款类型看，居民存款增加
2.4 万亿元，同比少增 5600 亿元。居民存款少增主要受两方面因素驱动：一
是债券市场阶段性走强， 部分居民存款向表外资管产品分流； 二是居民预期改

善乏力、提前还贷现象仍然存在。非金融企业存款增加 1.31 万亿元，同比多
增 3906 亿元，新型政策性金融工具加速投放，带动配套资金流入企业账户。
财政存款减少 4300 亿元，同比少减 4100 亿元。9 月政府债券净融资同比多增

### Page 3

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分
3
约 3438 亿元，资金先流入财政账户导致财政存款同比少减，扣除这一因素后
仍少减约 662 亿元（4100 亿元-3438 亿元），指向财政资金拨付落地偏慢。
非银行业金融机构存款减少 1.91 万亿元， 同比多减 8500 亿元，是9 月存款端
最大拖累项。非银存款明显少增，主要因季末理财资金季节性回表，但回表规
模不及去年同期；此外，9月权益市场交投走弱、赚钱效应收敛，居民存款向
非银产品的分流节奏明显放缓。从货币端来看，9月末，M2 同比增速收窄 0.3
个百分点至 7.2%， 低于wind 一致预测水平 7.45%。本月贷款增速的持续下行
直接制约了存款货币的创造，政府债券融资规模大幅上升、 财政存款同比少减，
也在短期内压低了 M2 增速。M1 同比增速收窄 0.9 个百分点至 3.2%，低于
wind 一致预测水平 3.9%， 主要因基数扰动叠加资金活化不足。9 月末，M2-M1
同比剪刀差扩大 0.6 个百分点至 4.0 个百分点，反映出内需偏弱背景下企业投

资意愿和居民消费意愿均有待进一步提振。随着政府债券资金加快拨付、 配套
融资逐步落地，企业存款有望获得支撑，但信贷增速下行仍将制约货币派生。
M2 有望保持一定韧性，M1 回升则取决于实体需求改善，M2-M1 剪刀差短期
或维持高位。
 风险提示：政策落地节奏不及预期风险，海外经济超预期波动风险。已公布的
宏观或产业政策在实际执行过程中，可能因配套措施不完善、部门协调不畅、
地方执行力度差异或市场环境变化等因素，导致其推进速度、覆盖范围或最终
效果慢于或弱于市场预期，从而影响政策目标的实现与经济修复的进程； 主要
经济体的政治局势、经济数据或宏观政策出现意外变化，其幅度或频率超出市
场普遍预测。这种超预期波动可能引发全球金融市场动荡、 贸易条件恶化或外
需急剧收缩，并通过金融、贸易及预期渠道向国内传导，增加我国外部环境的
复杂性与不稳定性。

分析师承诺  
本报告署名分析师具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格并注册为证券分析师，报告所采用的数据均
来自合法合规 渠道，分析逻辑基于分析师的职业理解，通过合理判断得出结论，独立、客观地出具本报告。分析师承
诺不曾因，不因，也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点而直接或间接获取任何形式的补偿。

投资评级说明  
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准， 新三板市场以三板成指 （针对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。  
公司评级  
买入：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 20%以上  
持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -10%与 10%之间  
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -20%与-10%之间  
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 -20%以下  
行业评级  
强于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报高于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 5%以上 
跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%以下

CAPEX&折舊 
- 2026 年 CAPEX 上調至 US$60-64bn(原為 US$52-56bn，接近上緣)；70~80% 於先進製程，10% 特殊
製程、10~20% 先進封裝、測試光罩 
- 資本支出通常對應未來數年更高的成長機會，目前看未來三年 CAPEX 會比過去三年再更為強勁 
- 已提前與設備供應商規畫產能，目前不預期設備供應會成為擴產計畫的重大瓶頸  
- 宣布將在亞利桑那追加 US$100bn 投資(總額提高至 US$265bn)，新增數座 N2 及更先進製程邏輯晶
圓廠、先進封裝廠 
AI 
- 先前提出的 2024-29 年 AI 相關營收「中高 50% CAGR」展望，管理層表示需求已變得「更強、更
強、再更強」，因為展望持續在調高所以不公布數字，實際趨勢高於先前說法  
先進封裝 
- 目前主流方案仍為 CoWoS，同時持續開發其他替代技術以降低封裝成本 
- 對 Intel EMIB-T 等先進封裝競爭，管理層認為後段和前段是完全不同等級，競爭者取得封裝訂單不
代表可切入前段邏輯晶圓

### Page 1

M
Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary August Profit and
September Monthly Sales (-31%
MoM/+196% YoY)
Sentiment to stay soft in near term but 2027 outlook is
promising.
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for August, per TWSE regulatory
requirements:
• Revenue was NT$6,466mn (+1% MoM/+344% YoY), 33% of our estimate for
3Q26 of NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 39% of consensus' NT
$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).

• Pretax income was NT$4,826mn (+216% YoY; pretax margin at 74.6%), 29%
of our 3Q26 pretax income estimate of NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and
34% of consensus' NT$14,094mn (margin at 84.1%).
• Net income was NT$3,819mn (+212% YoY; EPS NT$40.07; NPM 59.1%), 30%
of our 3Q26 net income estimate of NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM
66.2%) and 34% consensus' NT$11,118mn (EPS NT$114.38; NPM 66.4%).
September monthly sales
• September sales of NT$4,464mn (-31% MoM/+196% YoY), driving 3Q26

revenue to NT$17,338mn (+60% QoQ/+297% YoY), were short of our
estimate of NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and but ahead of the
consensus forecast of NT$16,755mn (+55% QoQ/+284% YoY).
Our view:
• We think the sequential decline in September  revenue was driven by slower
momentum for some projects, which entered the later part of their product
cycles. While sentiment should stay soft in the near term, we think the
outlook into 2027 is still promising as customers start new product cycles.

• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness in the comprehensive
patent portfolio and strong execution are entrenched and accentuated amid
fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027e P/E).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 125: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가삼성전자(005930) 현저한 저평가현저한 저평가 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

삼성전자 (005930/KS) 
현저한 저평가 
3Q26 영업이익 106 조원 예상 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익을 106조원 (+18% QoQ)으로 예상한다. 이는 기존 당사 
전망치 111 조원 대비 4.5% 하향된 것이다. 원/달러 환율 가정을 기존 1,450원에서 
1,400원으로 3.4% 하향, MX/NW 사업부의 적자 폭을 확대 반영 (-0.5조원 -> -
1.4조원)했기 때문이다. 주가의 핵심인 메모리 판가는 DRAM +17%, NAND +15%
로 오히려 예상을 소폭 상회할 것으로 예상된다. 출하는 수요 강세에도 불구하고 재고 
부족, HBM 확대에 따른 제한적 공급 여력으로 DRAM +4%, NAND +7%의 기존 예
상을 유지한다. HBM은 분기 최대 출하를 경신하기 시작할 것이다. 또한 AI 강세로 파
운드리 수주 및 가동률의 동반 상승이 지속 중이다. 성과 충당금 반영 등에 따라 단기 
흑자전환 가시성은 제한적이나, DS 사업부 전체적으로 강화되고 있는 국면이다.

2027 년 568 조원 (+55% YoY) 전망 유지. 환율 하락 반영 
SK증권은 삼성전자의 2027년 영업이익 전망치를 568조원으로 유지한다. 원/달러 환
율 가정을 기존 1,440원에서 1,340원으로 7% 하향했음에도, 실적 전망을 유지하는 
이유는 AI 메모리 수요 강세 속 공급 부족 심화, LTA에 따른 Dual market 효과에 의
한 Commodity 메모리 가격 강세, 2027년 HBM 판가 강세 (+103% YoY) 와 출하 
강세, 수익성 회복의 동반, 파운드리 적자 폭 축소의 본격화 전망 때문이다. 2027년 메
모리 출하는 DRAM +22%, NAND +16%, 판가는 DRAM +38%, NAND +28%
를 예상한다. 시장에서 우려하는 메모리 가격 하락, 감익 시나리오는 없을 것이다.

차별적 실적과 주주환원 본격화. 현저한 저평가 
SK증권은 메모리 업황과 삼성전자의 주가가 현저히 저평가 되어있다고 판단한다. 삼성
전자의 2027E P/E는 4.2X에 불과하며, 이익 창출력은 글로벌 최상위권이다. 주주환
원 정책 또한 기존의 FCF 50% 환원이 향후에도 유지될 것으로 가정한다면, 2027년 
193조원, 2028년 222조원으로 현재 시가총액 (우선주 포함) 대비 각각 11%, 13% 
수준에 육박한다. 성장을 위한 투자를 확대하고 있음에도, 대규모의 환원이 가능하다. 
LTA는 AI 시대 메모리 수요의 질과 실적 가시성을 제고시키며, 이는 주주환원의 지속
가능성을 의미한다. 메모리 가격의 높은 기저, LTA 물량의 계약 가격 적용을 감안하면, 
가격 상승률 자체는 안정화될 것이다. 다만 실적 안정성, 가시성, Duration을 확보하기 
시작했다는 점이 훨씬 중요한 가치이며, 아직 시장의 Playbook에 없을 뿐이다. 시대는 
변하고 있고, 시장도 변해야 한다. 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 610,000원을 유지한다. 
2026-10-06

반도체. 한동희 / donghee.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-8826

VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh

[Cập nhật công ty]

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn 
chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX đư ợc kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ gần 
quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dệt may, Tiện ích khí và Bao bì & đóng gói là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị  
-2,865 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã VHM, VPB và VIC; đồng thời, tự doanh đảo chiều bán 
ròng ở mức -146 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -3,351 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 1,650 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 2,822 hợp đồng nhưng 
đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,636 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam bước vào tuần đầu tiên ở vị thế thị trường mới nổi bằng một nhịp điều 
chỉnh, khi VN-Index để mất mốc 1.800 điểm và lùi về đóng cửa quanh 1.785 điểm. Đây là 
phản ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" khá điển hình: ngay trong phiên nâng hạng chính 
thức 21/9, chỉ số mở cửa hưng phấn rồi nhanh chóng đảo chiều do áp lực chốt lời, với 
nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup (VIC, VHM, VRE) giảm mạnh dẫn dắt đà đi xuống, và sức ép lan 
rộng sang bất động sản, ngân hàng, năng lượng về cuối tuần. Điểm đáng chú ý là dòng 
vốn ngoại đã tiếp tục bán ròng ngay sau cột mốc ngày 21/9, dù đã mua ròng trở lại trong 
tuần trước đó. Bối cảnh bên ngoài cũng kém thuận lợi khi Fed vừa tăng lãi suất và phát đi 
tín hiệu diều hâu, chứng khoán Mỹ điều chỉnh, giá dầu neo cao, và lợi suất trái phiếu 
Chính phủ Mỹ tăng vọt. Trong một, hai tuần tới, bên cạnh các yếu tố ngoại biên, mùa báo 
cáo KQKD quý 3 cũng sẽ bắt đầu tác động lên diễn biến thị trường chung.

### Page 3

Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ tăng 
trưởng khả quan trong 2Q26 14% 
YoY, với động lực chính đến từ mảng 
dịch vụ CNTT nước ngoài

Mảng CNTT nước ngoài: Lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ IT phân cực mạnh mẽ do tác nhân 
AI (AI Agentic) s ẽ củng c ố xu hư ớng 
đầu tư vào AI

FPT ở vị thế thuận lợi cho phép 
doanh nghiệp đa dạng hóa công cụ 
AI và tối ưu chi phí vận hành

Khuyến ngh ị MUA với giá m ục tiêu 
86,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Do không còn h ạch toán FPT Telecom (FOX) là công ty con, d oanh thu 2Q2026 của 
FPT giảm mạnh còn 13,810 tỷ VND (-17% YoY). Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ không 
bị ảnh hưởng bởi thay đổi hạch toán đạt 2,568 tỷ VND (14% YoY). Mảng dịch vụ CNTT 
tại thị trường nước ngoài tiếp tục là đ ộng lực tăng trư ởng chính, đạt doanh thu và 
LNTT lần lượt là 9,868/1,538 tỷ VND (16.3% /19.3% YoY).

Sự chuyển dịch chi tiêu từ dịch vụ IT truyền thống sang chuyển đổi số và tích hợp AI 
được kỳ vọng sẽ diễn ra ngày m ột mạnh mẽ khi: i) Sự phát triển của năng l ực tính 
toán giúp kéo giảm chi phí token (đơn v ị năng lực tính toán của AI) và thúc đ ẩy nhu 
cầu sử dụng AI của doanh nghiệp; ii) Các mô hình AI mã nguồn mở gia tăng lựa chọn 
cho người dùng sẽ thúc đẩy chi tiêu

Đối với các doanh nghiệp ứng dụng sâu các mô hình AI phục vụ hoạt động kinh doanh 
như FPT , chúng tôi nhận thấy xu hướng tối ưu chi phí tốt hơn nhờ khả năng sử dụng 
các mô hình AI đ ến từ đa dạng nhà cung c ấp; với chi phí trên hi ệu năng tính toán 
được cải thi ện. Trong k ịch b ản cơ s ở, chúng tôi d ự báo biên l ợi nhuận trước thu ế 
mảng CNTT nước ngoài sẽ ổn định ở mức 15.4% nhờ duy trì chi ến lược quản trị chi 
phí hiệu quả qua đó giúp LNTT 2026/2027 đạt 6,198/7,087 tỷ VND (13.3%/14.3% YoY).

Dựa trên định giá FCFF và P/E, triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các rủi ro có 
thể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu FPT . Giá mục tiêu là 86,000 
VND/cổ phiếu.

Các mảng kinh doanh duy trì ổn định 
giúp lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ 
tăng trưởng nhẹ

Sản lượng điện của REE tăng trưởng 
nhẹ nhờ mức tăng của mảng điện gió 
sau khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 vận 
hành

Mảng M&E ghi nhận giá trị ký mới cao 
trong 1H.2026, t ạo kỳ vọng hồi phục 
mạnh mẽ doanh thu

Mảng cho thuê văn phòng đứng trước 
cơ hội lớn đóng góp vào tăng trư ởng 
nhờ các điều kiện thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
58,100 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu Quý 2/2026 giảm nhẹ, đạt 2,432 tỷ VND (-3% YoY/-2% QoQ), trong khi l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ đạt 632 tỷ VND (+1% YoY/-11.5% QoQ), do doanh thu của 
mảng bất động sản suy giảm. Qua đó, REE hoàn thành lần lượt 40%/47.8% kế hoạch 
doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ 2026.

Trong Q2.2026, tổng sản lượng phát đi ện của REE tăng nh ẹ, đạt 2.8 tỷ kWh (+2.3% 
YoY) với đóng góp tăng trưởng rõ nét đến từ mảng điện gió khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 
bắt đầu vận hành; tuy nhiên  sản lượng thủy điện suy gi ảm do tác đ ộng tiêu c ực từ 
pha thời tiết El Nino, đạt 1.1 tỷ kWh.

Với năng lực chuyên môn được nâng cao, mảng M&E ghi nhận những dấu hiệu phục 
hồi tích cực trong giá trị hợp đồng ký mới trong 1H.2026, 5,865 tỷ VND (cao hơn 53.9% 
so với giá trị ký mới cả năm 2025). KBSV dự phóng doanh thu c ủa mảng M&E sẽ đạt 
4,499/5,190 tỷ VND (+28%/15% YoY) trong 2026/2027.

Hội tụ bởi 3 yếu tố: i) Định hướng phát triển kinh tế Hồ Chí Minh trở thành trung tâm 
tài chính quốc tế; ii) Hạ tầng giao thông ngày một được hoàn thiện và iii) Chi phí thuê 
cạnh tranh so với chất lượng dịch vụ của E. Town 6, KBSV dự phóng doanh thu mảng 
cho thuê văn phòng trong 2026/2027 tăng trưởng +14%/6.2% YoY.

Dựa trên đ ịnh giá SOTP , triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các r ủi ro có th ể 
phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu REE. Giá m ục tiêu là 58,100 
VND/cổ phiếu, cao hơn 28.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa tại ngày 15/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q.26 bứt phá nhờ khoản 
thu nhập bất thường của nhiệt điện 
Cà Mau và Vũng Áng

Nhiệt đi ện than duy trì công su ất ở 
mức cao nh ờ nguồn cung nhiên li ệu 
ổn định trong 2H26

Điện khí Đông Nam Bộ (NT1&2 và 
NT3&4) có triển vọng hoạt động 
thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
18,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Trong Quý 2/2026, POW ghi nhận Doanh thu và LNST cổ đông công ty mẹ đạt lần lượt 
20,308 tỷ VND (116.1% YoY) và 3,519 tỷ VND (484.6% YoY) chủ yếu nhờ các khoản thu 
nhập từ chênh lệch tiền khí của nhiệt điện Cà Mau và tỷ giá của nhiệt điện Vũng Áng. 
Với kết qu ả trên, POW hoàn thành l ần lư ợt 65%/420% kế hoạch doanh thu và l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ.

Chúng tôi cho r ằng nhà máy nhi ệt than Vũng Áng trong 2026 s ẽ được duy trì công 
suất huy động ở mức cao nhờ: i) Xác suất diễn ra El Nino tăng cao và dự kiến kéo dài 
đến đầu 2027, ii) Nguồn cung than trong nước được bảo đảm từ các đối tác (TKV, TCT 
Đông B ắc) và d ự trữ than d ồi dào c ủa doanh nghi ệp ghi nh ận ở mức cao trong 
Q2/2026.

Dự án đường dây 500kV Mạch 3 đi vào vận hành giúp giảm áp lực cạnh tranh của các 
nhà máy điện tại khu vực phía Nam, qua đó kỳ vọng doanh thu của NT1&2 trong năm 
2026 đạt 11,104 tỷ VND (5.3% YoY). Đối với dự án điện NT3&4, khoản thu nhập chênh 
lệch sản lượng theo giá công suất do sản lượng điện thực tế thấp hơn so với tỷ lệ Qc 
65% của nhà máy được duy trì sang 2H26 và 2027 sẽ giúp cho dự án ghi nhận doanh 
thu lần lượt 17,848/17,621 tỷ VND doanh thu trong 2026/2027.

Dựa trên định giá SOTP và EV/EBITDA, tri ển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các 
rủi ro có th ể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu POW. Giá m ục 
tiêu là 18,100 VND/cổ phiếu.

động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng ch ờ thương gia. Lũy k ế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 1,035/221 t ỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác tăng 
bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế gia tăng 
được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi khách, đặc biệt 
tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 182 
điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp t ừ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nh ất và 6 điểm mới tại T2 
Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Qu ốc và Gia Bình ti ếp tục mở ra 
dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, phòng 
chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh đ ược kỳ vọng tác động 
tích c ực lên biên l ợi nhu ận của AST . Chúng tôi dự phóng biên l ợi nhu ận g ộp đ ạt 
64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 48%/17% 
yoy.

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng hơn; 
trong khi tri ển v ọng nhóm khách 
hàng công nghi ệp b ị ảnh hư ởng b ởi 
bất lợi thuế quan

Đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyện và xử 
lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho phép 
cải thiện doanh thu và lợi nhuận của 
mảng này

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 
tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An v ận hành từ Q3/2025 giúp 
sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài c ủa các đ ợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ s ở để 
chúng tôi đi ều chỉnh tăng tăng trư ởng tiêu th ụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phư ớc trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế quan 
có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xu ất khẩu truyền thống của Việt 
Nam.

UBND TP .HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng mạnh

nghiệp ở mảng này s ẽ vận hành hi ệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và l ợi nhuận gộp

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX điều chỉnh trở lại trong tuần qua sau khi gặp thử thách tại vùng cản quanh 1850, 
hình thành cây nến tuần giảm điểm nhưng với phần bóng dưới khá dài cho thấy sự tham 
gia tích cực của lực cầu bắt đáy tại vùng hỗ trợ gần. Trên khung ngày, nhịp giảm mạnh giữa 
tuần đã khiến chỉ số đánh mất đáy ngắn hạn tuần trước và chớm cắt xuống dưới SMA200, 
trong khi phiên hồi phục cuối tuần chưa cho thấy sự cải thiện rõ rệt của lực cầu. Mặc dù 
diễn biến này cho thấy áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng 
tăng ngắn hạn chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại.

## Document 197: Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds

Source PDF: all report pdf/Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Compelling risk-reward in 
US 10-year bonds  
Summary 
Event: The US 10 -year government bond yield has surged 
past 5.0% to a 19-year high and has now entered our three -
month forecast range of 5.0-5.25%. At the time of writing, the 
10-year yield is trading at 5.2%, its highest since June 2007’s 
high of 5.32%. The next resistance levels are the 2002 high 
of 5.47% and the 2001 high of 5.53%.  
We view the risk/reward for the US 10-year government 
bond after the recent yield spike as  compelling and we 
are initiating a long opportunistic idea at the current 
yield. As the table on the right shows, if the 10 -year yield 
rises to 6%, investors would face less than 1% in negative 
total returns over a one-year period, whereas if the yield falls 
below 5.0%, they would gain at least 6% in total returns.  
While we continue to prefer the 3-7-year bond maturity range 
for income generation (the US 5-year bond yield is also 
above 5%), and broadly prefer corporate over government 
bonds amid solid corporate fundamentals, the US 10 -year 
government bond yield has finally reached reasonable levels 
for investors looking for slightly longer maturities. 
Rationale: We expect the US 10-year government bond yield 
to ease to 4.75-5.0% over a 12-month period due to: 
Easing oil prices and inflation over next 12 months: While 
the yield is likely to remain elevated in the next three months 
due to elevated oil prices caused by geopolitical uncertainty, 
heavy AI investment-driven bond issuance and a resilient US 
economy, it is likely to ease towards the 4.75 -5.0% range 
over 12  months as oil prices moderate either due to a 
resolution in the Middle East or a rerouting of Middle East 
energy supplies and as the impact of tariffs fade.  
We expect s hallower Fed rate hikes than market s: We 
expect a total of 50bps of Fed rate hikes by June 2027, before 
a cut in H2 as US inflation eventually eases towards the Fed’s 
2% target. This  rate trajectory is shallower than c. 95bps of 
further hikes priced by markets over the next 12 months. 
 The benchmark US 10-year government bond yield has 
risen to its highest since 2007, offering medium-term 
investors a compelling risk-reward trade-off 
US 10-year government bond yield and previous highs

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

At current levels, the US 10-year government bond yield 
offers investors compelling risk-reward trade off

year Developed Market government bonds from shifts in 
their yields; grey dotted lines roughly denote current spot 
yield levels

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Investor positioning adjustment: In the very near-term, the 
US 10 -year bond yield has room to move lower as hedge 
funds using systematic strategies (CTAs) are extremely short 
US government bonds, raising the chance of short -covering 
if upcoming jobs and inflation data miss estimates. 
A shallow Fed hiking cycle  
We expect the shallowest Fed hiking cycle in almost 
three decades. This should enable the US 10-year yield to 
ease towards 4.75 -5.0% over a 12 -month period. Following 
this month’s increase, we forecast two further 25bp hikes by 
June 2027, followed by a cut in H2 2027. Markets price at 
least three more hikes by June and a strong probability of a 
fourth by September. Our projected 75bp s of total hikes this 
cycle compares with 525bps of hikes in 2022–23, 225bps in 
2015–18, 425bps in 2004–06 and 175bp in 1999–2000. The 
last smaller mid-cycle move was a single 25bps hike in 1997. 
Why we expect a more cautious Fed 
The latest hike restored the Fed’s credibility after renewed 
oil-price pressures, elevated inflation and a balanced labour 
market prompted a unanimous policy vote. However, the 
following factors argue against aggressive tightening: 
Supply-driven inflation: Energy costs and AI-infrastructure 
constraints, rather than excess demand, are driving prices. 
Higher rates cannot repair supply and may weaken growth. 
Labour is no longer the main inflation source:  Labour 
supply and demand are better balanced, wage growth has 
moderated and unemployment is near full employment. AI 
further weakens the traditional Phillips Curve relationship 
between unemployment and inflation. 
Disinflation should resume:  Energy pressures may fade 
after the US mid-term elections, while the one-off inflationary 
effects of tariffs should diminish after 2025–26. 
AI raises the neutral rate , but dampens inflation:  
Investment and productivity may lift the neutral rate ( r*), the 
rate consistent with stable inflation and full employment, 
while efficiency gains limit wage-price spirals. 
Markets are already tightening  financial conditions : 
Higher bond yields have raised borrowing costs and 
restrained activity, doing part of the Fed’s work. 
The cycle began from a higher base:  The Fed funds rate 
started at 3.75%, versus 0.25% in the previous two cycles 
and 1% before that, reducing additional tightening required. 
Monetary transmission has weakened:  Wealthier 
households drive more spending but are less rate -sensitive, 
while lower -income consumers are already constrained. 
Further hikes may , therefore, curb demand less effectively 
while causing disproportionate harm to low-income families. 
 We expect a shallower Fed rate hiking cycle compared 
with market expectations 
Our policy rate outlook for the Fed, ECB and BoJ

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *dotted lines reflect our 
expected rates path over the next 12m

While near-term inflation is lifted by oil prices and 
tariffs, long-term inflation expectations remain subdued

expectations

Small business conditions are deteriorating:  Higher 
financing and energy costs are weakening sentiment, as 
reflected in the latest Beige Book . The renewed 
underperformance of US small capitalisation (S&P 600) and 
mid capitalisation (S&P 400) equity indices  versus the large 
cap S&P 500 index over the past couple of months also show 
rising pressure on small businesses. 
Fiscal and financial -stability risks are rising:  Aggressive 
Fed tightening would increase US debt-servicing costs as the 
US Treasury seeks cheaper funding through shorter-maturity 
issuance, while potentially amplifying financial-system stress. 
Upside risk to bond yields 
Our view of lower bond yields carries the following risks: 
Persistent inflation:  Repeated supply shocks, strong AI -
related investment demand and tighter immigration controls 
could keep inflation above target and force steeper Fed rate 
hikes. With unemployment near the Fed’s estimate of full 
employment, a sharp drop in labour supply could intensify 
wage pressures and destabilise inflation expectations . We 
note that long-term expectations remain contained for now. 
Geopolitical escalation:  An intensification of geopolitical 
conflicts after the mid -term elections could lift energy prices 
and inflation. However, a Democrat -controlled or divided 
Congress may constrain US foreign-policy initiatives through 
tighter budgets, while Republicans could turn less supportive 
as attention shifts to the 2028 presidential race. 
Higher neutral rate: AI investments could raise the neutral 
rate (r*), turning current monetary policy settings 
accommodative. This could necessitate further rate hikes. 
However, the Fed has barely raised its nominal neutral rate 
estimate, lifting it to 3.2% in September from 3.0% a year ago. 
Bottomline: The outlook for rates remains geopolitics 
dependent, with all Fed meetings live . However, based on 
our core scenario, we see the US 10-year yield settling lower 
in the 4.75-5.0% in 6-12 months. A shallow Fed hiking cycle 
due to expected disinflation offers investors a reasonably 
attractive risk-reward in US 10-year government bonds. 
US 30-year government bonds , on the other hand, may 
continue to see elevated yields as the US fiscal deficit is 
unlikely to have improved next year, and may look much 
worse. As a result, “term premium” may keep the ultra -long 
end elevated. Short of some unorthodox policy to artifici ally 
cap them lower, the 30-year yield is likely to continue to drift 
higher towards the 5.5-5.75% range into H2 2027. 
— Jonathan Liang, CIO for Fixed Income and FX   
Rajat Bhattacharya, Senior Investment Strategist 
Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Acter, an established 
multinational cleanroom engineering, procurement, construction and 
management (EPCM) turnkey service provider, with a Buy (1) rating. Its 
core business includes cleanroom electromechanical engineering, 
water/gas/chemical delivery systems, and other cleanroom services. We 
believe now is a good time to revisit the stock as it underperformed the 
TAIEX Index by 32.7% in 3Q26, mainly on weaker-than-expected 2Q26 
results and soft sector sentiment. Given its stronger revenue momentum 
over recent months and upcoming TSMC results on 15 October, which may 
drive sentiment, we suggest investors to buy the stock. We forecast a 
24.6% revenue CAGR and 36.9% net profit CAGR over 2026-28E.

Intact global demand for cleanroom technology, the edge AI market, 
and rapid cleanroom expansion in Asia further support Acter’s order 
backlog. Its backlog reached TWD66bn in July 2026 vs. TWD46bn in 2025 
and TWD30bn in 2022 (15.3% 2022-25 CAGR), which largely outpaced the 
global cleanroom technology market’s forecast CAGR over 2023-2032E, 
according to Precedence Research.

Strong competitive edge supports order share gains across multiple 
segments. Acter’s competitive edge lies in 1) intelligent management 
enabled by digital twins and AI, using 3D building information modelling 
(BIM) and AI-driven digital twin management; 2) intellectualisation of green 
engineering, which comprises green design, green procurement, and green 
techniques; 3) experience across multiple industries (ie, data centres and 
electromechanical engineering for the consumer industry) which supports 
better integration; and 4) a complete network, enabling on-the-spot support. 
Acter believes it has the highest market share among the operators of three 
major US data centre players due largely to its capabilities in liquid cooling 
and its expertise in electromechanical and cleanroom engineering.

Catalysts: Near-term: project wins from key clients, supporting stronger 
monthly revenue momentum; mid- to long-term: an improving operating-
margin trend and a resilient order backlog.

Valuation: We are the first non-domestic broker to cover Acter. We initiate 
coverage with a 12-month TP of TWD1,415, based on a target PER of 20x 
(equivalent to a 0.54x PEG over 2026-28E). We believe our valuation is 
fair, given Acter’s strong earnings visibility, which we believe warrants a 
rerating. Our TP is supported by a DCF valuation of TWD1,454.

Risks: 1) weaker-than-expected expansion in the semiconductor industry; 
and 2) project delays or cancellations.

How do we justify our view?

Growth outlook

Acter: net profit and net-profit growth    
We forecast Acter to record a 36.9% net profit CAGR over 
2026-28E, on the back of a 24.6% revenue CAGR over the 
same period, driven by 1) larger project scale, as strong 
demand for cleanroom EPCM services from the high-tech 
sector is likely to support continued growth in Acter’s order 
backlog and 2) improving gross margins and operating 
efficiency. We expect operating margin to expand by 2.0pp 
in 2027E and 1.3pp in 2028E, driven by favourable gross 
margins on new projects and operating leverage.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Acter: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Our 12-month TP of TWD1,415 is based on a target PER 
of 20x, applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Our 
target multiple of 20x is higher than the stock’s past-3-year 
1-year-forward PER (12.2x) and past-5-year 1-year-
forward PER (9.6x) and implies a PEG of 0.54x on our 
36.9% net profit CAGR forecast over 2026-28E. We 
believe the stock deserves a rerating in the next 12 
months, largely supported by resilient cleanroom demand 
globally.

Our TP is also supported by an alternative DCF-derived 
valuation of TWD1,454/share.

Company profile 
Established in 1979, Acter Group (Acter) is a leading engineering services provider specialising in 
cleanroom, electromechanical, and high-tech facility integration solutions for semiconductor, 
optoelectronics, biotech, green energy, and advanced manufacturing clients across Taiwan, China and 
the rest of the world. As of 2025, its revenue comprised cleanroom electromechanical (71%), water, gas 
and chemical delivery systems (20%), consumer industry electromechanical (4%), biomedical (2%) and 
high-tech equipment & materials sales & services (3%).

### Page 5

5

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Acting on the demand for cleanrooms 
Why are cleanrooms a bottleneck in the high-tech 
industry? 
In recent years, cleanrooms have become a critical bottleneck for high-tech industries, 
especially semiconductor manufacturing, OSAT, AI hardware, and PCB, mainly because of 
its indispensable nature, severe supply-demand imbalance, labour shortages, and high 
technical and regulatory barriers.

Cleanrooms are an indispensable core infrastructure as they determine product 
yield and quality stability. Advanced manufacturing nodes in semiconductors, 
optoelectronics, and biopharmaceuticals require highly particle-free, constant-temperature, 
and constant-humidity environments to ensure reliable production. As process 
technologies continue to advance, such as 2nm node (or even newer nodes) and 
advanced packaging, existing cleanrooms must be rebuilt, and the complexity of building 
new ones thus increases. This keeps overall demand for cleanrooms at high levels.

A severe global supply-demand imbalance with demand for cleanroom construction 
outpacing supply. The surge in AI servers, high-performance computing (HPC), and high-
bandwidth memory (HBM) has triggered a global expansion for front-end wafer fabs, 
packaging and testing facilities, and PCB manufacturing. Order visibility remains extremely 
strong across the industry. According to Markets and Markets, the global cleanroom market 
size is set to expand at a CAGR of 6.6% over 2025-31E. At the same time, the global 
semiconductor cleanroom market size is set to grow at a CAGR of 8.0% over 2025-30E. 
For instance, Acter’s order backlog exceeded TWD66bn at the end of July 2026, up from 
TWD46bn at the end of 2025, showing the urgency of demand for cleanroom construction.

Global: cleanroom market size and growth      Global: semiconductor cleanroom market size and growth

Source: Markets and Markets  Source: Markets and Markets

Human resources are an additional limitation to capacity growth. Cleanroom 
engineering is highly technical and labour-intensive, relying on specialised on-site 
installation and supervision that cannot be replaced by robots. At the same time, the 
industry faces severe labour shortages and an aging workforce, and younger generations 
are reluctant to join field operations. Cross-border staff deployment is also less cost-
efficient than local hiring.

Cleanrooms involve complex system integration, creating high barriers to entry. A 
cleanroom construction includes 4 sequential phases, where each phase usually requires 
totally different areas of expertise. Therefore, the competitive landscape in each area 
differs. The process begins with civil & structural (C&S) works to establish the physical 
plant, followed by mechanical, electrical, and plumbing (MEP) works for essential utility 
generation, where Acter is one of the leading global experts. After MEP is finished, 
chemicals/gas/water are then distributed via main systems through primary piping 
network and finally delivered to production equipment through hook-up systems. As

Cleanrooms are a 
bottleneck in the high-
tech industry, including 
semiconductor foundry,

### Page 6

6

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
global supply chains restructure under N+1 (domestic market + 1) strategies, companies 
building overseas facilities in places like the US face strict and complicated licensing 
processes, particularly on fire safety and hazardous chemicals. Without experience dealing 
with such engineering and safety issues, construction timelines can be severely delayed.

As a result, even with advanced equipment and chip designs, a high-tech company cannot 
begin production without adequate cleanroom space, skilled engineers, and system-
integration capabilities. Therefore, cleanroom engineering contractors hold significant 
bargaining power and capacity allocation leverage in today's tech supply chain.

IC foundry 
In the foundry sector, where the cleanroom supply-demand shortage is the tightest, project 
activity remains centred on advanced process capacity expansion in Taiwan and Arizona. 
Simultaneously, domestic advanced node development remains intense, with multi-phase 
fab builds advancing across Hsinchu, Taichung, and Kaohsiung. Local cleanroom and MEP 
integration demand is further boosted by advanced packaging facilities in Taiwan (such as 
Chiayi and Tainan) and upcoming projects in the US are set to contribute revenue for the 
cleanroom providers in the following years.

Memory 
The memory segment is exhibiting robust momentum as well, mainly catalysed by HBM 
capacity additions. Projects such as the plant in Japan, transitioned to contribution for the 
cleanroom EPCM providers starting from 2026. In Taiwan, turnkey engineering contracts in 
Miaoli are also actively underway. This trend is reinforced by stronger-than-expected 
expansion activity from memory producers in China.

OSAT,  PCB and ODM/OEM 
For OSAT companies, demand is also surging due to the spill-over effect of advanced 
packaging protocols and N+1 supply chain restructuring. The OSAT names are expanding 
their footprint both in Southern Taiwan and in overseas locations including in the US and 
Southeast Asia. Major engineering contracts signed between OSAT clients and Acter show 
stable long-term workload for strong cleanroom demand over our forecast horizon. 
Similarly, overseas expansion for PCB manufacturing is peaking in Southeast Asia, 
especially in Thailand. We expect Chinese PCB players to also increase their capacity, just 
like memory companies in the past year. Except for front-end and back-end chip 
manufacturing, ODM/OEM and data centre infrastructure is also a key growth engine for 
the EPCM industry. Major Taiwanese ODMs are building production facilities in Texas and 
Mexico to meet local manufacturing needs. Cleanroom contractors, such as Acter, 
providing MEP and system integration in Texas are directly benefiting from these projects. 
The shift toward liquid cooling systems has also increased engineering complexity, 
favouring few cleanroom companies with the expertise to deliver complex integration for 
CSPs in both North America and Southeast Asia.

Acter: an integrated cleanroom solutions provider  
Acter Group (Acter), an integrated cleanroom solutions provider, maintains its competitive 
positioning within the high-tech cleanroom and MEP engineering sector, driven by its group 
synergy and global footprint. Acter operates as a comprehensive vendor through a group 
business model. The parent company, Acter, supervises overall cleanroom and MEP 
services, while subsidiary Nova Technology (Novatech, 6613 TT, not rated) mainly provides 
chemical primary system and hook-up, and Novatech’s subsidiary Rayzher (7703 TT, not 
rated) provides gas primary system, hook-up and equipment. In China, Acter operates 
through Acter Technology Integration Group (603163 CH, not rated) and Suzhou Winmax 
(not listed) for Chinese EPCM (engineering, procurement, construction, and management) 
and equipment business. Except for C&S (civil and structural) works, where the margin is 
low (7-8%) and the contract duration is long, Acter’s services span nearly the entire 
cleanroom construction process. Its one-stop solutions model supports customer retention 
Acter provides 
integrated cleanroom 
solutions via various 
subsidiaries

### Page 7

7

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
and improves bid-win rates, particularly for overseas expansion, where customers have 
few reliable providers to choose from.

Acter: organisational chart

Source: Company, Daiwa

To avoid the risk of over-concentration, Acter diversifies its client portfolio across 
semiconductor foundries, memory, PCB, OSAT, ODM hardware assembly, CSP data 
centres and the consumer industry. By sector, semiconductor projects represent the largest 
share of Acter’s portfolio, accounting for 66% of total revenue in 1H26, vs. 69% in 2025. 
Foundry manufacturing is the largest source of demand for cleanrooms in Taiwan, 
especially as advanced-node capacity expands at sites in Hsinchu, Taichung and 
Kaohsiung. In the memory segment, the company executes turnkey engineering builds to 
expand its capacity aggressively, creating cleanroom opportunities across Taiwan, Japan 
and Singapore. This multi-sector coverage decreases the risk of cyclical downturns in any 
individual segment, in our view.

By establishing 33 operating entities across 11 international regions, the company 
effectively captures capex migration driven by global N+1 supply chain diversification. This 
allows Acter to support Taiwanese and international clients as they expand manufacturing 
capacity from Taiwan into Southeast Asia, Japan and the US. Due to the complexity of 
advanced-node IC manufacturing, packaging and testing, it is especially important for 
these Taiwanese semiconductor companies to work with familiar cleanroom builders when 
expanding in the US. This is one of the reasons why Taiwanese cleanroom companies are 
receiving overseas orders and starting to set up global bases, in our view. Despite higher 
management, material, and manpower costs in the US, most cleanroom companies are 
benefitting from the trend by improving margins, given the higher complexity and risks to 
operate overseas. Across overseas hubs in Texas and Phoenix (expected to begin from 
2027), Acter prioritises local hiring and partners with regional civil contractors to shorten 
the learning curve and to avoid the high overhead. To further address the potential labour 
shortages in the future, the company has established cross-border academic and 
vocational partnerships to bring Southeast Asian talent to Taiwan for technical training 
before deploying them back to their home markets, securing future human capacity to 
support global expansion projects.

Acter provides 
cleanroom services for 
numerous industries, 
across multiple regions

By product, cleanroom electromechanical services remain Acter’s largest source of 
revenue (accounting for 66% of 1H26 revenue, per the company). These services include 
cleanroom architectural and MEP engineering, which powers airflow, filtration, 
environmental regulation and utility controls. Water, gas and chemical delivery systems are 
its second-largest source of revenue (accounting for 24% of 1H26 revenue, per the 
company). Contributed mainly by Novatech (6613 TT, not rated) and Rayzher (7703 TT, not 
rated), these services cover the main systems and hook-up systems, for  ultra-pure water 
(UPW), specialty gases, chemical piping, etc., all of which require highly precise build-up 
work. Except for cleanroom electromechanical and water, gas and chemical delivery 
systems, the rest of Acter’s revenue comes from the consumer industry services 
electromechanical (5%), biomedical (3%), and high-tech equipment and materials sales 
and services (1%).

Acter: revenue by product

Source: Company, Daiwa

Technically, Acter integrates digital transformation and green engineering into its business 
model, helping it differentiate from peers. Acter deploys digital twin technology and 1:1 3D 
BIM modelling to detect piping interferences and optimise spatial layout before installation, 
minimising schedule delays. This makes Acter a more reliable option, in our view, and thus 
being a favourable supplier, especially for the semiconductor clients that have extremely 
high quality and swiftness criterion.

Bright outlook: strong order visibility 
Rising orders on hand due to supply-demand imbalance for cleanrooms 
Acter's order backlog has increased from TWD55bn in 1Q26 to TWD60bn in 2Q26 and 
TWD66bn in July 2026. We expect this figure to further increase in the following quarters, 
given the supply-demand imbalance in the cleanroom sector. This is composed of 
semiconductor (63%), electronic assembly (11%), consumer (9%) and others (17%), per 
the company. In 2H25, the company accelerated the digestion of small-scale projects 
below TWD1bn, and, as a result, starting from 1H26, it successfully shifted its project

(MEP) integration, especially for those data centres that implement liquid-cooling systems. 
The company also wins factory construction projects for Taiwanese ODMs across the US 
and Southeast Asia, as well as PCB facility projects in Thailand and China. Non-tech 
sectors, including biotechnology, commercial buildings, smart logistics hubs and medical 
facilities, comprise 9% of the total backlog, per management.

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Competitive advantages 
High entry barriers to the segment 
Comprehensive product portfolio, empowered by subsidiaries 
Acter provides a one-stop EPCM solution, leveraging its subsidiaries to deliver complete 
solutions. Within the group, the parent company Acter focuses on overall cleanroom and 
MEP system integration; subsidiary Novatech provides primary chemical systems and 
hook-up systems; and sub-subsidiary Rayzer specialises in primary specialty-gas systems 
and hook-up systems. In China, Suzhou Winmax, also a subsidiary of Novatech, focuses 
on the supply of wet processing and chemical handling equipment. This integrated model 
covers cleanroom engineering integration, water, power, chemical and gas-utility systems, 
as well as post-completion validation and maintenance, ensuring cross-system 
compatibility while mitigating risks associated with multi-contractor hiring.

Under global supply chain diversification trends, this unified group strategy strengthens 
order-winning capabilities in overseas markets. Clients expanding internationally face 
regulatory, linguistic and supply-chain complexities, favouring partners that offer one-stop 
services with a proven cross-border track record.

A diverse client base helps Acter weather economic cycles 
Acter’s main competitive advantage is its diversification of clients by industry and region. 
This guides the company through the peaks and troughs of the economy, which is 
especially crucial given the strong cyclicality of the semiconductor/high-tech industry.  Acter 
serves a broad client base across semiconductors, OSAT, PCB, electronics assembly 
(ODM), CSP data centres, biopharmaceuticals and even the consumer industry (including 
commercial real estate and logistics), whereas many peers rely on a few large foundry 
clients. When semiconductor demand slows, capacity expansion in the PCB or data centre 
sectors offset the decline. Additionally, Acter avoids concentration risk and margin 
compression from major foundry accounts, as projects from leading foundries yield lower 
margins; by serving niche markets like US-based CSPs, the company achieves higher 
gross margins.

Geographically, Acter operates across Taiwan, China, Southeast Asia, the US and other 
countries. The company has OSAT and foundry customers in Taiwan and China, PCB 
clients expanding into Thailand, and ODM and data centre clients establishing operations 
in the US. Across different countries, local hiring and local supply chain implementation 
can further reduce costs and increase efficiency relative to peers reliant on a cross-border 
labour force.

Labour shortages 
Amid global labour shortages, Acter minimises the operational impact and turns workforce 
constraints into a moat through localised talent development. Since engineering labour 
cannot easily be automated, Acter has built a cross-border talent pipeline via industry-
academia partnerships. The company recruits Southeast Asian students for specialised 
M&E engineering training in Taiwan, deploying them to global project sites after their 
graduation to solve labour bottlenecks. This structure maintains an engineering team with 
an average tenure of more than 7 years, creating an operational barrier in an industry with 
a high employee turnover rate.

Compared with peers relying on high-cost personnel sent from Taiwan, we note that Acter’s 
local hiring strategy creates cost and efficiency advantages. Prioritising compliant local 
hiring across overseas markets reduces travel expenses while accelerating integration with 
local supply chains and regulations. Localised teams also handle post-completion services 
and technical support directly on-site, building long-term client trust in overseas markets.

The supply of cleanroom 
services is limited due to

### Page 11

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Numerous competitors, each with different specialisation 
Acter faces different competitors across its main product lines. In the EPCM segment, 
including cleanroom and MEP works, key competitors include UIS (2404 TT, not rated), 
L&K (6139 TT, not rated), Yankey (6691 TT, not rated) and Jiuhan (6903 TT, not rated). 
Despite having business models similar to Acter, UIS, Yankey and Jiuhan focus more on 
major semiconductor players in both Taiwan and the US, and L&K concentrates on ASEAN 
semiconductor projects. In the equipment/material sales and agency space, Acter mainly 
competes with Marketech (6196 TT, not rated). In the water, gas and chemical delivery 
systems area, through Novatech and Rayzher, Acter group competes with Marketech, 
Mega Union (6944 TT, not rated), Taiwan Puritic (6826 TT, not rated), Trusval (6667 TT, not 
rated) and Cica-Huntek (6725 TT, not rated).

In our view, despite the competitive environment, Acter maintains a formidable competitive 
moat built on several key advantages. We attribute this to its more than 45 year experience 
in the industry, enabling it to develop specialised expertise that is hard for competitors to 
replicate.

Financial analysis 
We forecast the company’s net profit to rise at a CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E, driven by 
a revenue CAGR of 24.6% due to surging demand from the high-tech industry (an increase 
in the order backlog to TWD66bn at the end of July 2026 vs. TWD46bn at end-2025) and a 
3.3pp expansion in operating margin over 2026-28E, driven by the gross margin 
improvement from more favourable project wins and operating leverage.

Strong order visibility, with revenue expected to grow 
across all sectors over 2026-28E 
We forecast Acter’s revenue to increase at a CAGR of 24.6% over 2026-28E, driven by 
scale expansion and a growing demand from various industries, including semiconductor, 
electronic assembly, optoelectronics and others.

Acter: revenue growth trend

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Gross- and operating-margin expansion trend  
Over 2026-28E, we forecast Acter’s gross and operating margins to expand on rising 
revenue scale and a higher complexity of cleanrooms for advanced manufacturing. We 
note that the gross margin from high-tech cleanrooms is higher than that from consumer 
industries, such as commercial buildings and biopharmaceuticals. Thus, we estimate the 
company’s gross margin to reach 26.0% in 2028E from 18.9% in 2025 and its operating 
margin to reach 21.6% in 2028E from 13.7% in 2025.

Acter: gross- and operating-margin trend

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Net margin likely to rise over 2026-28E 
We forecast Acter’s net profit to rise to TWD8,797m in 2027E and TWD11,851m in 2028E 
(from TWD6,325m in 2026E). We expect its net margin to come in at 13.1% in 2027E and 
14.2% in 2028E (vs. 11.7% in 2026E) and for its net profit to grow at a CAGR of 36.9% 
over 2026-28E.

Intact demand to support revenue growth over our 
forecast horizon; ROE expansion underway 
We forecast Acter’s total cash and equivalents to reach TWD16,838m (36.8% of its total 
assets) in 2026E and increase to TWD27,839m in 2028E (42.8% of total assets) with net

We believe the capacity expansion cost will initially be low and that the company will 
continue to benefit from the better profitability.

We expect Acter’s ROE to improve to a record-high of 56.8% in 2028E from 29.3% in 2025 
(following steady annual increases to 46.8% in 2026E and 54.9% in 2027E), which we 
attribute to margin expansion from the revenue size increase and gross margin expansion 
led by increases in cleanroom complexity.

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Valuation and risks  
Undemanding valuation, alongside its leading global 
positions in the OSAT and memory segments and an 
optimistic industry outlook  
We initiate coverage of Acter with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD1,415, based 
on a target PER of 20x applied to our one-year-forward EPS forecast. This target multiple 
is higher than the average of its past-3-year 1-year-forward PER of 12.2x (ranged from 
7.4x to 23.5x) and the average of its past-5-year 1-year-forward PER of 9.6x (ranged from 
4.4x to 23.5x), which we view as undemanding as we forecast a 36.9% net profit CAGR 
over 2026-28E, implying a 0.54x PEG. This is supported by its business expansion in the 
next 2-3 years. We see potential for a rerating of the stock, given Acter’s global leadership 
in cleanroom EPCM, particularly in the OSAT and memory industries.

The stock is trading currently at a 15.8x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 
36.9% over 2026-28E, translating to a 0.43x PEG ratio. Its share price has underperformed 
TAIEX Index by 32.7% in 3Q26, mainly due to its weaker-than-expected 2Q26 results. 
However, given the potential improvement in sector sentiment following TSMC’s results on 
15 October 2026, along with the likelihood that Acter’s revenue momentum will strengthen 
in the coming months, we recommend that investors buy the stock.

Acter: past-3-year-forward PER (average: 12.0x)    Acter: past-5-year-forward PER (average: 9.5x)

Source: Bloomberg, Daiwa estimates  Source: Bloomberg, Daiwa estimates

We compare Acter with other leading cleanroom EPCM companies such as UIS (2404 TT, 
TWD1,330.0, not rated), L&K (6139 TT, TWD787.0, not rated) and Yankey (6691 TT, 
TWD776.0, not rated). UIS, L&K and Yankey are trading at a PER of 14.7x (earnings 
CAGR of 40.7% over 2025-27E), 9.5x (earnings CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E) and 
15.1x (earnings CAGR of 53.6% over 2025-27E), on their 1-year-forward EPS, 
respectively.

Acter’s peers are trading at 18.7x for 2026E and 14.2x for 2027E. We forecast Acter to 
deliver an EPS CAGR of 36.9% over 2026-28E vs. 31.5% CAGR for the segment over the 
same period.

We believe our target one-year-forward PER of 20x (equivalent to a 0.54x PEG using 
2026-28E EPS CAGR) is undemanding as: 1) Acter is ramping up capacity to meet 
growing demand from high-tech customers and is in an expansion cycle; and 2) its 
leading position among OSAT and memory clients warrants a valuation premium, 
compared to its global peers.

Risks to our call  
Primary/key risks: 
Weaker-than-expected expansion in semiconductor industry 
The biggest downside risk to our call for Acter is weaker-than-expected expansion from 
semiconductor clients, especially in advanced-node foundries, advanced packaging and 
OSAT. If global demand for AI servers, HPC or HBM slows, or if clients delay fab build-ups 
and overseas expansion plans, it will directly pressure order inflows and backlog 
conversion for Acter.

Project execution delays or cancellations 
Acter’s revenue recognition is tied to large, complex EPCM and integrated engineering 
projects, which are often subject to customer-driven delays. Any postponement in project 
Our 12-month TP of 
TWD1,415 is also 
supported by our DCF 
valuation

### Page 18

18

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
schedule, construction bottlenecks or cancellation of large projects could defer revenue 
recognition and hurt overall profit. This risk is more obvious in overseas projects, where 
licensing, fire-safety approvals and hazardous-chemical regulations are more complicated.

Higher-than-expected raw-material price hikes 
Raw-material costs (especially copper and oil-related costs) account for 50-60% of COGS 
and thus the price volatility makes costs difficult to manage. Acter needs to maintain 
supplier relations, negotiate appropriate price-adjustment mechanisms with clients, and 
invest in R&D to identify composite material substitutes, thereby reducing reliance on any 
single component.

Secondary risks:  
Labour issues or larger-than-expected increases in labour costs  
Cleanroom engineering is highly labour-intensive and relies on on-site installation and 
supervision. Although Acter has built a local hiring and talent-development model, a larger-
than-expected shortage of engineers or rising wage pressure could still raise costs and 
constrain capacity expansion. Any inability to secure enough skilled labour may also limit 
the company’s ability to convert orders into revenue in time.

Margin pressure from lower-margin projects or mix shift 
Acter’s business mix includes a few low-margin C&S services as well as larger EPCM and 
system integration contracts. If the company takes on a higher proportion of lower-margin 
projects, or if competition intensifies in certain geographies or customer segments, gross 
margin could come under pressure. In addition, an unfavourable product mix shift towards 
less profitable work could offset benefits from backlog growth.

Overseas expansion and regulatory risks 
Acter is expanding across markets in the US, Southeast Asia, Japan and China, which 
exposes it to FX movements, local compliance requirements, tax complexity and regulatory 
uncertainty. Delays in licensing, changes in safety standards or local-content requirements 
could increase project costs and reduce execution efficiency. Any misstep in managing 
overseas operations could damage profitability and client retention. 
Key risks to our call:

expansion in

cancellations, and 
higher-than-expected

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Appendix 
Company background 
History 
Acter (5536 TT), founded in 1979 and headquartered in Taichung, Taiwan, is a cleanroom 
EPCM provider with over four decades of experience. Starting with early mechanical and 
electrical (M&E) engineering through vertical and horizontal integration, Acter has now 
developed into an engineering technology group with cross-border execution capabilities. A 
cleanroom building process begins with civil & structural (C&S) works to establish the 
physical plant, followed by mechanical, electrical, and plumbing (MEP) works for 
essential utility generation, where Acter is one of the experts. After MEP is finished, 
chemicals/gas/water are then distributed via main systems through primary piping 
networks, and finally delivered to production equipment through hook-up systems. The 
company’s core competency is providing one-stop integrated total solutions, spanning 
services, primary piping networks and hook-up systems, leaving C&S services to other 
engineering peers, given the nature of the latter segment’s low margin and long timespan.

In terms of business model, Acter delivers EPCM (Engineering, Procurement, 
Construction, Management) capabilities. From initial customised planning and feasibility 
assessments to strategic procurement, installation, and post-completion maintenance, 
Acter builds production environments tailored to client process requirements.

Acter: EPCM solutions offered

Source: Company, Daiwa

Regarding global expansion, Acter actively responds to the global supply chain’s “N+1” 
strategy, operating across 11 regions with 33 companies globally. Its service network spans 
Taiwan, China, Southeast Asia (including Thailand, Malaysia, Vietnam, Indonesia, and 
Singapore), Japan, India, and the US. The company leverages a group synergy strategy 
by integrating resources from key subsidiaries – such as Novatech (6613 TT, not rated; 
provides chemical primary and hook-up systems), Rayzher (7703 TT, not rated; provides 
special gas primary and hook-up supply systems), and Acter Group (603163 CH, not rated; 
provides cleanroom engineering in China) – to form a complete technical ecosystem, 
ensuring cross-border capability and quality.

Comprehensive 
cleanroom EPCM 
provider in Taiwan; 
dominates both high-
tech market and 
consumer industry

### Page 20

20

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
Acter: global footprint

Source: Company, Daiwa

In 2Q26, Acter classified revenue as: cleanroom electromechanical (63%), water, gas and 
chemical delivery systems (28%), consumer industry electromechanical (6%), biomedical 
(2%) and high-tech equipment & materials sales & services (1%). In 2025, the product mix 
was: cleanroom electromechanical (71%), water, gas and chemical delivery systems 
(20%), consumer industry electromechanical (4%), biomedical (2%) and high-tech 
equipment & materials sales & services (3%).

Acter serves a diversified client base divided into high-tech and consumer divisions. The 
high-tech division focuses on advanced sectors including semiconductors, OSAT, PCB, AI 
data centres (for CSPs), and electronics assembly. The consumer division extends into 
healthcare, premium commercial properties, logistics facilities, and green engineering. This 
multi-industry strategy allows the company to capitalise on tech sector upgrades while 
maintaining resilient operations across technology (especially semiconductor) cycles.

Acter adopts a management strategy involving local hiring in overseas markets, prioritising 
collaboration with local supply chains and compliance with local regulations to reduce 
cross-border deployment costs and thus build strong local teams. Acter has more than 
2,600 employees worldwide (average age: 42 years; average length of service: 7 years). 
With more than 45 years of experience in EPCM, the company plays a reliable role for both 
high-tech and consumer industry.

recycling, and AI smart facility management systems to help clients achieve carbon 
neutrality while expanding capacity. Backed by solid corporate governance, Acter

Glossary of terms 
EPCM (engineering, procurement, construction, and management): Cleanroom 
construction services that cover the whole process including project design, purchasing, 
construction and management.

BIM (building information modelling): A digital modelling system of a building and its key 
information used in cleanroom construction.

C&S (civil & structural): Site infrastructure and the design and construction of load-
bearing elements.

MEP (mechanical, electrical, and plumbing): Building essential systems for environment 
control, power, lighting, water, and drainage.

Acter (5536 TT): 7 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 Oct 2026 
Source:  Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Acter maintains a structured governance framework that supports disciplined management. The
chairman is Chin-Li Liang. The company operates with defined responsibility across the board,
management, cyber security oversight, operations, and audit functions. It has established an
organisation function led by the President, a Chief Cyber Security Officer, a Response Team, an
Operations and Policy Enforcement Team, and an Audit Team to supervise policy execution and
review security implementation.
Capital management 1
Acter manages capital in a stable and shareholder-friendly manner. Over the past several years,
Acter has typically maintained a dividend payout ratio of 65-80%. It also maintains a strong
financing network across multiple banks, supporting flexibility for project execution and
expansion while preserving financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Acter’s business is supported by a transparent network of suppliers, project partners, and
financial counterparties, including companies with equity ties and reciprocal business
relationships that are common in its operating ecosystem. These relationships are managed
through formal internal procedures for fund lending, endorsements and guarantees, and asset
acquisition and disposal, all of which are approved through shareholder mechanisms and
company rules. Such a structure helps keep inter-company and supply-chain transactions
organised and traceable, reducing the risk of unclear dealings.
E Product quality &safety 1
Acter executes its projects through a complete, disciplined process covering bidding
assessment, project planning, design, procurement, construction management, verification and
testing, completion and acceptance, closing review, and warranty service. Its cleanroom and
electromechanical engineering business is built around maintaining temperature, humidity, and
dust-free environments that support high-precision manufacturing. The company strengthens
quality control through BIM, Revit, simulation software, ISO drawing output tools, and automated
modeling, which improve design accuracy, construction efficiency, and project consistency.
E Ecological impacts 1
Acter has built a concrete sustainability programme with measurable environmental outcomes.
The company completed Scope 1, 2, and 3 greenhouse gas emissions calculation, obtained
third-party verification under ISO 14064-1:2018, and publicly disclosed energy consumption and
emissions data. It also implemented a strong waste reduction programme, achieving a 95%
wastepaper recycling rate at headquarters in 2025 and 100% paper recycling at the 19F and 20F
floors after establishing paper segregation and reuse procedures. The company also promoted
sustainability measures through emissions inventory management, environmental disclosures,
and resource-efficiency practices. These actions show active environmental management and a
clear commitment to lowering operational impact.
Health & safety

險，油價延續上週漲勢 ，儘管美國總統川普
表示美伊衝突即將結束 ，然中東地緣擾動短
期內無改善跡象，恐致油價維持高位難降。
► 中東衝突令通膨壓力增 溫，推升聯準會升息
預期，美元轉強 。歐洲央行如期升息一 碼，
歐元持平 。日本銀行官員增田和幸表示應進
一步升息 ，確保通膨不高於政策目標 ，美國
財政部長施壓日本升息預期 ，日圓較主要貨
幣顯強，未來留意主要央行政策動向。

► ECB於9月10日把三項政策利率各上調 25個
基點，存款利率將於9月16日升至2.50%。央
行指出中東 衝突持續 製造通膨壓力，同時警
告增長下行風險 。ECB升息後，市場已訂價
明年4月升息超過75基點。
► 日 銀 下 周9 月17 至18 日 開 會， 委 員 高 田 創
(Hajime Takata)警告，在金融條件仍寬鬆 、
日圓偏弱及能源與食品成本上升下 ，若基礎
通膨加速 ，央行或須較 快升息；他認為現時
1%的政策利率仍然偏低 。不過，他亦表示通
膨未有急升，故毋須一次加50個基點行動。
► 全球面對能源供給衝擊推高 通膨，利率上升
影響收入和需求 。ECB已選擇先行抑制第二
輪通膨效應 ，代價是融資成本及成長股折現
壓力； 日銀 若 確 認 升息， 則進一 步支 持日
圓、推高日本公債 收益，加速日圓套利交易
平倉。日圓轉 強的情況下 ，我們預期銀行及
精選保險機構 、公用事業 、進口主導零售／
必需消費品、本土電信與服務業或能受惠。

投資焦點美國公債
► 美國財政部 9月10日執行10年期與20年期公
債回購計畫，並擴大回購規模從20億增至60
億美元 ，欲對缺乏 流動性的長天期舊債進行
流動性支持 ，減輕公債拋售 潮引發期限 溢價
走升問題 ，然實際回購規模僅 51.8億美元並
低於預期的 60億至100億美元令市場失望 。
財政部亦公布例行 10年與30年期公債標售結
果，宣布執行擴大回購操作後 ，從投標倍數
與得標 減預期利率 差距來看 ，反映長 天期公
債需求不淡 ，然川普承諾 共和黨若在期中選
舉中獲勝 ，將向全國成年 公民發錢，恐導致
未來公債 供給量續升 ，後續視 美國政府能否

維持財政紀律逐步恢復市場信心。
► 美國非農新增就業數明顯較前月改善 ，反映
就業市 場韌 性 ， 體現 實體 經濟表 現優 於 預
期，然短期 通膨難 回政策目標，預期聯準會
最快9月重啟升息 ，2年期公債利率水準已反
映升息空間 ，考量財政問題 與美國升 息預期
同在 ，投資美國公債宜慎 ，留意Fed官員態
度與經濟預估變化。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國經濟未見明顯疲態！為聯準會埋下升息伏筆財政部倍增長期公債回購不如預期，然標售需求回溫
美財政部倍增公債回購不如預期，保守看待公債配置

### Page 7

投資焦點
► 2026年第2季美國標普500企業財報數據顯示
獲利成長明顯高於債務增速 ，另可發現代表
獲利護城河的大型企業毛利率貼近 2022年以
來高位，淨利率亦創1998年以來新高 ，資本
支出年增至 25%歷史高位水準 ，另資本 支出
佔EBITDA比率升至 35%，預期美國大型企
業持續擴大資本 支出推動獲利 成長動能 ，同
時維持獲利高於資本開支的資本效率 ，避免
大額資本支出過度失控 ，造成未來債信體質
與償債能力惡化。
► 從今年1月至8月美國各天期投資級債表現來
看，可以發現中短天期債含息總報酬仍維持
正值，主因美國政府財政 問題與升息擔憂推
升殖利率曲線上行 。從短、中、長天期殖利
率/存續期間比率來看 ，美國短天期債具備利
率波動下最大抗震能力 ，而長天期承受 利率
上行耐受幅度相對僅 50個基本點 ，考量利率
風險可能持續至年底 ，債市投資人可配置中
短天期債券降擾動。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國中短天期投資級債年至今表現相對較穩美國大型企業淨利率創1998年來新高

美國大型企業債信仍強，中短天期投資級債為首選

### Page 8

8
股市
 全球主要國家債務攀升 ，利率創高添股市評價修正風險 ，加上科技股舉債投入資本支出 ，未來獲利
與變現能力備受關注 。美伊衝突加劇不確定性 ，短線可透過布局醫療與公用事業等防禦股 ，降低市
場震盪風險 ，科技及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利等基本面支持長線發展 ，採逢回分批布局 ，並
分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，日本半導體股
分批布局較穩健，歐洲國防軍工仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融、工業等產業機會
區域：日本銀行股、日本半導體股、歐洲
金融、國防
債市
 發債供給與聯準會升息預期 ，美國公債殖利率續升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國10
年期公債利率將於4.5%-5.0%震盪，可逢利率走升時 ，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體
質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者 ，包含金融、科技、通訊及公

用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會9月升息預期持續，美元短線高檔震盪。
 日本銀行釋出鷹派訊號，9月升息可能性升高，有利日圓止貶回升。
日圓震盪偏升
商品
 中東衝突反覆 ，荷姆茲海峽短期恢復正常通行機率偏低 ，加上石油庫存探低 ，預期今年能源仍呈供
不應求態勢，油價維持偏高水位。
 美國財務壓力與去美元化之兩個長期結構性問題依然存在，央行買盤提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金區間操作，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 債務供給與升息預期擾動，基本面形塑中長期布局契機

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。
